# Stage 04
## Blocks
| Block | Guide section | Learning points / contexts | Cards | ID range |
|---|---|---|---:|---|
| B2a | 6.5, 5 | Nuanced aspect: general-factual versus completed-event report, experience, cancelled result, repeated completion. Interviews, reviews and everyday narrative; event/result cue must be explicit. P; B1 narrative aspect. | 100 | ru-04-0001–ru-04-0100 |
| B2b | 6.5, 5 | Aspect in argument, modal/negative environments, intention/attempt and pragmatic commands. Proposals, expectations and professional requests; visible scope/timeline and task result. P; A2 commands, B1 hypotheses. | 100 | ru-04-0101–ru-04-0200 |
| B2c | 6.5, 5 | Wider literal prefixed motion: routes, multiple legs, carrying/leading/flying and result versus regular route; lexicalized partner relations. Travel, distribution and role-play. P; B1 prefix sequence; distinguish prefix meaning from manner/directionality. | 100 | ru-04-0201–ru-04-0300 |
| B2d | 6.5, 5 | Figurative motion in decisions, argument, time and information: approaching agreement, reaching conclusions, transitions and digression. Context selects extended sense; include accurate government. P; literal senses in B1/B2c, no transparent-prefix assumption. | 100 | ru-04-0301–ru-04-0400 |
| B2e | 6.5, 9 | Full active/passive participial constructions, tense/voice, agreement, case and restrictive/descriptive relation. Reports, notices and reviews; controller/agent clear. P written/R dense genre form labeled; B1 recognition and relative clauses. | 100 | ru-04-0401–ru-04-0500 |
| B2f | 6.5, 13 | Adverbial participles with prior/simultaneous action, perfective/imperfective and negation; appropriate written/formal-spoken usage. Procedures, proposals and contemporary narrative. P/R explicitly scoped; same-subject relation and timeline visible, no dangling control. | 100 | ru-04-0501–ru-04-0600 |
| B2g | 6.5 | Passive, reflexive-passive, impersonal and active alternatives; agent suppression versus named responsibility. Process reports and services; retain affected participant/agency in meaning-preserving edits. P; B1 voice/impersonals. | 100 | ru-04-0601–ru-04-0700 |
| B2h | 6.5 | Complex numerical phrases, dates/years, fractions, percentages, quantities and case government. Budgets, surveys, schedules and comparisons; short figures with full meaningful inflection, no compression to evade caps. P; A2 number frames. | 100 | ru-04-0701–ru-04-0800 |
| B2i | 6.5, 5 | Nominalization and formal written syntax: action noun frames, agent/recipient genitives, avoiding ambiguous chains. Applications, proposals and reports; relation clear. P; B1 government. Do not make all advanced output bureaucratic. | 100 | ru-04-0801–ru-04-0900 |
| B2j | 6.5, 8 | Colloquial particles and reductions, omitted but recoverable components, lexical casual/formal alternatives. Friends and ordinary workplace chat; register and omitted reference visible. P standard ordinary colloquial, R nonstandard reductions labeled; B1 particles. | 100 | ru-04-0901–ru-04-1000 |
| B2k | 6.5, 10 | Word order for contrast/emphasis/cohesion and scalar/restrictive scope. Reviews, proposals and corrections; given/new or contrast cue present in same short source. P; B1 focus, no arbitrary reorderings. | 100 | ru-04-1001–ru-04-1100 |
| B2l | 6.5, 5 | Productive derivational families around writing/recording/signing, decision/permission/resolution and related nouns/adjectives. Actual lexical senses, stress and collocations, not transparent root equations. P; familiar core verbs; legal/administrative specificity introduced in visible context. | 100 | ru-04-1101–ru-04-1200 |
| B2m | 6.5 | Additional noun/adjective/verb derivation and register-sensitive synonyms in work, social life, media and abstract evaluation. Hide distinct useful sense/frame; note false-friend risks when material. P; B2l and ordinary lexicon. | 100 | ru-04-1201–ru-04-1300 |
| B2n | 6.5, 12 | Polite disagreement, criticism, requests, concessions and negotiation. Professional role-play, service problems and group proposals; recipient/authority/commitment explicit. P; B1 social and conditional language. | 100 | ru-04-1301–ru-04-1400 |
| B2o | 6.5, 9 | Source comparison, evidence/claim distinction, argument and review collocations. Short self-contained contrast of two claims or one attributed limitation. P; reporting/hypotheses; no actual long-source-synthesis claim. | 100 | ru-04-1401–ru-04-1500 |
| B2p | 6.5, 8–10 | Frequent idioms and contemporary narrative collocations, literal/figurative interpretation, evaluation and stance. Everyday, professional and accessible cultural contexts; meaning cue visible. P/R by register; avoid obscure proverb lists and cosmetic repetition. | 100 | ru-04-1501–ru-04-1600 |

| ru-04-0001 | B2a | opened; Masculine singular past; кто takes masculine singular agreement here. Imperfective; contextual perfective partner открыть. Открывает is third-person singular present. Takes an accusative object; the question asks who performed the event, regardless of its current result. The closed window makes clear that the earlier opening no longer has a current result. |

| ru-04-0166 | B2b | respond; Plural or polite singular imperative. Perfective ответить; imperfective partner отвечать. Future ответит; takes a dative recipient. The imperative requests a specific response today. Клиенту identifies the recipient; the purpose clause explains the deadline. |

| ru-04-0250 | B2c | ran around; Past plural, agreeing with бегуны; озеро is the accusative object. Perfective; imperfective обегать in the circuit sense. Future обегу, обежит. Imperfective обегать here is distinct from the perfective homograph meaning to visit several places. |
| ru-04-0251 | B2c | rounded; Past masculine singular, agreeing with катер; мыс is accusative. Perfective; imperfective огибать. Future обогнёт. | ru-04-0253 | B2c | flew around; Past masculine singular, agreeing with вертолёт; гору is accusative. Perfective; imperfective облетать. Future облетит. Об- marks a completed route around the mountain by air. Unprefixed лететь and летать contrast directionality. |
| ru-04-0254 | B2c | wandered off in different directions; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with друзья. Perfective; imperfective разбредаться. Future разбредутся. | ru-04-0255 | B2c | left in different directions; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with друзья. Perfective; imperfective разъезжаться. Future разъедутся. Раз- marks completed departure in different directions by vehicle. Unprefixed ехать and ездить contrast directionality. |
| ru-04-0256 | B2c | carried apart; Past neuter singular, agreeing with течение; лодки is accusative. Perfective; imperfective разносить. Future разнесёт. По разным берегам specifies where the current scattered the boats. |
| ru-04-0257 | B2c | delivers to multiple addresses; Third-person singular present, agreeing with почтальон; письма is accusative. Imperfective; perfective разнести. Present разношу, разносят. | ru-04-0258 | B2c | deliver to separate addresses; Plural or polite singular imperative; коробки is accusative. Perfective; imperfective развозить. Future развезу, развезут. | ru-04-0259 | B2c | passed each other; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with два поезда. Perfective; imperfective разъезжаться. Future разъедутся. Поезда разъехались means that the trains passed each other, for example on separate tracks. |
| ru-04-0260 | B2c | pass each other; Reflexive perfective infinitive in a purpose clause; the two pedestrians are its implied subject. Perfective; imperfective расходиться. Future разойдутся. Here разо- marks people passing each other on foot; the infinitive expresses their intended result. Unprefixed идти is unidirectional and ходить multidirectional; both are imperfective. Разойтись here means to make room and pass someone moving in the opposite direction. |
| ru-04-0261 | B2c | ran down; Past feminine singular, agreeing with Анна. Imperfective сбегать in the descent sense; perfective сбежать. Present сбегает; the past describes repeated downhill runs. С- marks downward movement by running, and с takes the genitive. Unprefixed бежать is unidirectional and бегать multidirectional; both are imperfective. Downhill сбега́ла differs in stress and sense from colloquial perfective сбе́гала, a brief return errand; its future is сбе́гаю. |
| ru-04-0262 | B2c | take there and back by vehicle; Perfective infinitive governed by можешь; бабушку is animate accusative. Perfective; возить can report a trip without presenting the outing as completed. Future свожу, свозит. С- marks taking someone by vehicle on a return trip; к takes the dative. Unprefixed везти and возить contrast directionality. Свезти and свозить are different verbs: свозить here means to take someone somewhere and back. |
| ru-04-0263 | B2c | gathered by vehicle; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with мастера. Perfective; imperfective съезжаться. Future съедутся. С- marks completed convergence by vehicle at one event; на takes the accusative for the event and из the genitive for origins. Unprefixed ехать is unidirectional and ездить multidirectional; both are imperfective. На фургонах specifies the vehicles used; the participants converge at the exhibition. |
| ru-04-0264 | B2c | left the road; Past feminine singular, agreeing with машина. Perfective; imperfective съезжать. Future съедет. С- marks completed movement by vehicle off a surface or route; с takes the genitive and в the accusative. Unprefixed ехать and ездить contrast directionality. |
| ru-04-0265 | B2c | drove together; Past plural, agreeing with пастухи; коров is animate accusative plural. Perfective; imperfective сгонять in the gathering sense. Future сгонят. Со- marks completed convergence of driven animals; с takes the genitive for the sources and в the accusative for the enclosure. Unprefixed гнать is unidirectional and гонять multidirectional; both are imperfective. The herders' means of travel is unspecified. Согнать коров в один загон describes gathering the animals into one enclosure. |
| ru-04-0266 | B2c | take down; Perfective infinitive governed by помоги; коляску is accusative. Perfective; imperfective спускать. Future спущу, спустит. С- marks a completed downward transfer; по takes the dative for the stairs. The verb does not specify whether the stroller is carried or rolled. Спустить что-либо по лестнице describes moving an object down the stairs. |
| ru-04-0267 | B2c | were ascending; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with мы. Imperfective; perfective подняться. Present поднимаюсь, поднимаются. Под- marks upward movement, here on foot along a path; по takes the dative. The past presents the ascent as ongoing. |
| ru-04-0268 | B2c | took up; Past plural, agreeing with мы; чемодан is accusative. Perfective; imperfective поднимать. Future поднимем. Под- marks a completed upward transfer to an endpoint; на takes the accusative and по the dative. The precise way the suitcase was moved is unspecified. |
| ru-04-0269 | B2c | rose; Past masculine singular reflexive, agreeing with шар. Perfective; imperfective подниматься. Future поднимется. Под- marks completed upward movement through the air; над takes the instrumental. The past presents the rise before the next movement. |
| ru-04-0270 | B2c | took off; Past plural, agreeing with птицы. Imperfective; perfective взлететь. Present взлетают, взлетает. Вз- marks upward movement into flight; с takes the genitive for the starting surface. Каждый раз indicates repetition. Unprefixed лететь and летать contrast directionality. |
| ru-04-0271 | B2c | take; Plural or polite singular imperative; образцы is accusative. Perfective; imperfective относить. Future отнесу, отнесёт. От- marks taking objects away to a destination; в and через take the accusative. The command requests completed delivery; the full means of travel is unspecified. Unprefixed нести and носить contrast directionality. |
| ru-04-0272 | B2c | flew away; Past masculine singular, agreeing with мяч. Perfective; imperfective отлетать in the motion-away sense. Future отлетит. От- marks completed flight away after impact; к takes the dative for the direction. Unprefixed лететь is unidirectional and летать multidirectional; both are imperfective. The ball rebounds through the air toward the player after hitting the wall. |
| ru-04-0273 | B2c | will take by vehicle; Third-person singular simple future, agreeing with отец; велосипед is accusative. Perfective; imperfective отвозить. Future отвезу, отвезёт. От- marks transport away to a destination by vehicle; в takes the accusative. The future presents completed delivery. Unprefixed везти and возить contrast directionality. |
| ru-04-0274 | B2c | jumped aside; Masculine singular past; Олег is the subject. Perfective; imperfective отпрыгивать. First conjugation: отпрыгнет. The past reports a completed physical jump; от- marks movement away, and в сторону gives the direction. The jumping family has no идти/ходить-type directional pair. |
| ru-04-0275 | B2c | were moving away by vehicle; Past plural, agreeing with мы. Imperfective; perfective отъехать. Present отъезжаю, отъезжают. От- marks movement by vehicle away from a point; от takes the genitive. The past presents the departure as ongoing. Unprefixed ехать and ездить contrast directionality. |
| ru-04-0276 | B2c | went behind; Past plural, agreeing with мы. Perfective; imperfective заходить. Future зайдём. За- marks completed movement on foot into a position behind an obstacle; за takes the accusative. Unprefixed идти and ходить contrast directionality. Зайти за дом means to go to the far side of the house. |
| ru-04-0277 | B2c | drop off by vehicle; Second-person singular imperative; документы is accusative and брату dative. Perfective; imperfective завозить. Future завезу, завезёт. За- marks a completed delivery by vehicle at an intermediate stop. The imperative requests the delivery. Unprefixed везти and возить contrast directionality. Завезти документы брату means to drop the documents off with him on the way. |
| ru-04-0278 | B2c | led behind; Past masculine singular; feminine завела is also possible because проводник does not establish gender. Туристов is animate accusative. Perfective; imperfective заводить. Future заведёт. За- marks completed movement while leading people behind an obstacle; за takes the accusative. The means of movement is unspecified. Unprefixed вести and водить contrast directionality. |
| ru-04-0279 | B2c | reached by water; Past plural, agreeing with мы; visible не negates the result. Perfective; imperfective доплывать. Future доплывём, доплывёт. До- marks reaching an endpoint by water; до takes the genitive. The negated past says the endpoint was not reached. Unprefixed плыть is unidirectional and плавать multidirectional; both are imperfective. Плыли establishes a journey in progress; так и не доплыли states that it did not reach the island. |
| ru-04-0280 | B2c | having reached on foot; Perfective adverbial participle; its implied subject is the person addressed by поверните. Perfective; imperfective доходить. Future дойду, дойдёт. До- marks reaching an endpoint on foot; до takes the genitive. The participle places the completed first leg before the turn. Unprefixed идти and ходить contrast directionality. Дойдя до моста gives a completed action before the instruction to turn; this is controlled written usage. |
| ru-04-0281 | B2c | ran up; Past feminine singular, agreeing with Маша. Perfective; imperfective взбегать. Future взбегу, взбежит. Вз- marks upward motion by running; по takes the dative for the route. The past presents one completed ascent. Unprefixed бежать and бегать contrast directionality. |
| ru-04-0282 | B2c | rolled in; Past feminine singular reflexive, agreeing with тележка. Perfective; imperfective вкатываться. Future вкачусь, вкатится. В- marks entry by rolling; в takes the accusative. The past presents completed entry. Unprefixed катиться and кататься contrast directionality. |

| ru-04-0286 | B2c | came upon; Past plural, agreeing with туристы. Perfective; imperfective набредать. Future набреду, набредёт. | ru-04-0288 | B2c | flew into; Past feminine singular, agreeing with птица. Perfective; imperfective налетать. Future налечу, налетит. На- marks collision during flight; на takes the accusative. The past presents one completed impact. Unprefixed лететь and летать contrast directionality. |

| ru-04-0292 | B2c | gave a ride; Past masculine singular, agreeing with папа; дочку is animate accusative. Perfective; imperfective прокатывать in this ride-giving sense. Future прокачу, прокатит. | ru-04-0293 | B2c | slipped through; Past feminine singular, agreeing with кошка. Perfective; imperfective проскальзывать. Future проскользну, проскользнёт. | ru-04-0301 | B2d | moved closer; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with стороны. Perfective; imperfective приближаться. Future приближусь, приблизятся. К takes the dative. The past presents progress toward an agreement without asserting that agreement was reached; the approach is figurative. Приблизиться к соглашению means to make progress toward agreement. |
| ru-04-0302 | B2d | agreed; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with мы. Perfective; imperfective сходиться in this sense. Future сойдёмся, сойдутся. | ru-04-0303 | B2d | differ; Third-person plural present reflexive, agreeing with позиции. Imperfective; perfective разойтись can describe positions becoming divergent. Present расхожусь, расходятся. | ru-04-0304 | B2d | derived; Past masculine singular, agreeing with Павел; формулу is the accusative object. Perfective; imperfective выводить. Future выведет. Вывести takes an accusative result and из plus the genitive for its source. The past presents the derivation as completed. Вывести формулу из равенств means to derive a formula from equations; the motion is figurative. |
| ru-04-0305 | B2d | follows; Third-person singular present in an impersonal inference construction. Imperfective; no simple perfective partner in this logical sense. Present следует, следуют. Из takes the genitive for the evidence, and the что clause states the proposed inference. Не denies that the inference follows. Из данных следует, что introduces a conclusion supported by the data. |
| ru-04-0306 | B2d | proceeded; Past feminine singular, agreeing with Лена. Imperfective; no simple perfective partner in this assumption-based sense. Present исхожу, исходит. Из takes the genitive; из того, что introduces the assumption guiding the argument. The past describes her reasoning during the dispute. Исходить из того, что means to proceed on the assumption that something is true. |
| ru-04-0307 | B2d | move away; First-person plural simple future, used with давайте as a proposal. Perfective; imperfective отходить. Future отойду, отойдём. От takes the genitive for the topic left behind. The proposal invites a completed change of focus; the departure is figurative. Отойти от темы means to move away from the current topic. |
| ru-04-0308 | B2d | digress; Plural or polite singular reflexive imperative, negated by не. Imperfective; perfective отклониться. Present отклоняюсь, отклоняются. От takes the genitive for the subject of discussion. The negative imperative asks listeners to stay on topic; the movement is figurative. Отклоняться от вопроса means to stray from the question being discussed. |
| ru-04-0309 | B2d | began to make progress; Past neuter singular reflexive, agreeing with дело; мёртвой точки is genitive singular after с. Perfective сдвинуться; imperfective сдвигаться. The movement is figurative. |
| ru-04-0310 | B2d | turned; Past masculine singular, agreeing with разговор. Perfective; imperfective заходить in this discourse use. Future зайдёт, зайдут. О takes the prepositional; о том, как introduces the subject that came up. The past presents its emergence as an event. Разговор зашёл о чём-то means that a subject came up in conversation. |
| ru-04-0311 | B2d | came; Past neuter singular, agreeing with дело. Perfective; imperfective доходить. Future дойдёт, дойдут. До takes the genitive for the stage reached. The past presents the process as having reached that stage; the movement is figurative. Дело дошло до подписи means that proceedings reached the signing stage. |
| ru-04-0312 | B2d | reached; Past plural, agreeing with переговоры. Perfective; imperfective заходить в тупик. Future зайдут, зайдёт. В takes the accusative in the fixed phrase. The past presents negotiations as having reached an impasse. Зайти в тупик means to reach a point where progress seems impossible. |
| ru-04-0313 | B2d | showed understanding of my situation; Вошёл is past masculine singular; the same supervisor's перенёс establishes masculine agreement. Моё положение is accusative singular after в. Perfective войти; imperfective входить in this idiom. Future войдёт. В takes the accusative; the past presents one completed act of understanding. Войти в чьё-либо положение means to understand someone's difficult circumstances and take them into account. The entry is figurative. |
| ru-04-0314 | B2d | entail; Perfective infinitive governed by может. Perfective; imperfective влечь in this sense. Future повлечёт, повлекут. За собой is followed by the accusative consequence. The modal construction presents a possible result, not an actual one. Повлечь за собой задержку is a formal way to say that something may cause a delay. |
| ru-04-0315 | B2d | bring up to speed; Perfective infinitive governed by нужно; его is the animate accusative recipient outside the idiom. Perfective ввести; imperfective вводить. Future введёт. The construction takes an accusative recipient; в курс дела is fixed, with accusative курс and genitive дела. The infinitive expresses a needed completed briefing. Ввести кого-либо в курс дела means to acquaint someone with the information needed to participate. The introduction is figurative. |
| ru-04-0316 | B2d | spread; Past masculine singular reflexive, agreeing with слух. Perfective; imperfective расходиться. Future разойдётся, разойдутся. По takes the dative for the area in which the rumour spread. The past presents completed dissemination; no physical route is specified. Слух разошёлся means that a rumour spread among people. |
| ru-04-0317 | B2d | avoided; Past masculine singular; feminine обошла is also possible because руководитель does not establish gender. Perfective; imperfective обходить. Future обойду, обойдёт. Вопрос is the accusative object; стороной completes the idiomatic frame. The past presents one completed omission. Обойти вопрос стороной means to avoid addressing a question. |
| ru-04-0318 | B2d | redirected; Past feminine singular, agreeing with Ольга; разговор is the accusative object. Perfective; imperfective переводить. Future переведёт. Перевести разговор на takes an accusative object and на plus the accusative for the new topic. The past presents a completed redirection. Перевести разговор на другую тему means to redirect a conversation to another subject; the shift is figurative. |
| ru-04-0319 | B2d | expires; Third-person singular present, agreeing with срок. Imperfective; perfective истечь. Present истекает, истекают. The subject names a period of validity; the present states its scheduled end. The flow sense is extended to time. Срок действия договора истекает means that the agreement's period of validity comes to an end. |
| ru-04-0320 | B2d | was omitted; Past neuter singular, agreeing with условие. Perfective; imperfective выпадать. Future выпадет, выпадут. Из takes the genitive for the text from which information is omitted. The past presents the omission as completed, without identifying an agent. Условие выпало из сообщения means that a condition was left out of the message. |
| ru-04-0321 | B2d | made concessions; Past plural, agreeing with родители. Perfective; imperfective идти на уступки. Future пойдут на уступки. На takes the accusative; по- presents the concessions as a completed step. The motion is figurative. Пойти на уступки means to give up some demands to accommodate another person. |
| ru-04-0322 | B2d | accommodated her; Past feminine singular; изменила identifies the same director as female. Ей is dative. Perfective; imperfective идти навстречу. Future пойдёт навстречу. Навстречу takes the dative person; по- marks a completed accommodating response, without literal travel. Пойти кому-либо навстречу means to take someone's needs or wishes into account. |
| ru-04-0323 | B2d | draw a distinction; Perfective infinitive governed by важно; грань is feminine accusative singular. Perfective провести; imperfective проводить in this sense. The simple future includes проведёт. The verb takes accusative грань; между takes the instrumental for the things distinguished. The infinitive presents drawing the distinction as important. Провести грань между чем-либо distinguishes two concepts. Here the distinction is between criticizing an idea and insulting its author. |

| ru-04-0727 | B2h | eight thousand; Instrumental quantity phrase; книг is genitive plural after тысячами. Располагать governs the instrumental to name the resources available. |
| ru-04-0728 | B2h | one hundred and twenty-one; Animate accusative compound numeral; the final одного agrees with masculine singular музыканта. |
| ru-04-0729 | B2h | half a million; Genitive quantity form after около; рублей is genitive plural. Полумиллиона and полмиллиона are both standard genitive forms. The genitive полумиллиона has полу- in place of пол- in the dictionary form полмиллиона. Около marks the amount as approximate. |
| ru-04-0730 | B2h | seven hundred and fifty thousand; Genitive compound quantity phrase governed by недостаёт; рублей is genitive plural. Недостаёт names the amount still needed to reach the full sum. |
| ru-04-0731 | B2h | one to three; Один is the nominative form naming the first quantity; к governs dative трём for the second. The order of сиропа и воды gives one part syrup to three parts water. |
| ru-04-0732 | B2h | one third; Nominative singular feminine as the subject; дохода is genitive singular. Треть names the share of the family's monthly income spent on rent. |
| ru-04-0733 | B2h | two thirds; Instrumental fraction after с; заказа is genitive singular. Справиться с identifies the portion of the order completed. |
| ru-04-0734 | B2h | three fifths; Nominative fraction; платформы is genitive singular. The fraction identifies the share of the platform available to passengers. |
| ru-04-0735 | B2h | one eighth; Dative feminine singular fraction after distributive по; фонда is genitive singular. По assigns an equal share of the fund to each participant. |
| ru-04-0736 | B2h | one point two; Genitive decimal numeral governed by достигла; метра is genitive singular. Достичь names the measurement reached. |
| ru-04-0737 | B2h | two point five; Instrumental decimal numeral governed by ограничена; метра remains genitive singular. Ограничена identifies the maximum width of the passage. |
| ru-04-0738 | B2h | zero point one; Nominative decimal quantity phrase; грамма is genitive singular. |
| ru-04-0739 | B2h | one hundredth of a percent; Genitive feminine singular fraction governed by не превышает; процента is genitive singular. The fraction gives the upper limit for the share of defective products. |
| ru-04-0740 | B2h | four percent; Nominative quantity phrase naming the rate; процента is genitive singular. Годовых specifies that the interest rate is annual. |
| ru-04-0741 | B2h | sixty percent; Inanimate accusative quantity phrase after составила; процентов is genitive plural. От числа зарегистрированных избирателей identifies the population used as the base. |
| ru-04-0742 | B2h | ninety-eight; Inanimate accusative compound numeral after на; процентов is genitive plural. На with a percentage states the extent to which the plan is complete. |
| ru-04-0743 | B2h | ten percentage points; Inanimate accusative quantity phrase after составляет; процентных пунктов is genitive plural. Процентные пункты measure the arithmetic difference between two percentages. |
| ru-04-0744 | B2h | eight hundred; Genitive numeral after до; рублей is genitive plural. С ... до gives the original and new monthly prices. |
| ru-04-0745 | B2h | five hundred; Instrumental numeral governed by ограничен; рублями is also instrumental plural. The phrase states the upper limit on the contribution per person. |
| ru-04-0746 | B2h | one and a half million; Dative quantity phrase governed by равна; рублей is genitive plural. Равна introduces the amount of the payment. |
| ru-04-0747 | B2h | from one and a half to two; От and до each govern a genitive bound: полутора and двух belong to different numeral inflection patterns. Месяцев is genitive plural. The phrase gives the lower and upper bounds of the course duration. |
| ru-04-0748 | B2h | twenty-two fifteen; Inanimate accusative clock-time phrase after в; часа is genitive singular and минут is genitive plural. The full twenty-four-hour time identifies the scheduled departure as 22:15. |
| ru-04-0749 | B2h | twelve thirty; Genitive clock-time expression after с; both numerals decline. С ... до marks the beginning and end of the scheduled break. |
| ru-04-0750 | B2h | the twenty-fifth; Accusative neuter singular compound date ordinal after по; only the final component declines. По ... включительно includes the stated date in the application period. |
| ru-04-0751 | B2h | the twenty-sixth; Genitive neuter singular compound date ordinal after с; июня is genitive singular. С identifies the original date from which the meeting was moved. |
| ru-04-0752 | B2h | the twenty-ninth; Dative neuter singular compound date ordinal after к; сентября remains genitive singular. К gives the date by which the participants will receive the schedule. |
| ru-04-0753 | B2h | two thousand and thirtieth; Prepositional year expression after в; only the final ordinal declines and agrees with году. |
| ru-04-0754 | B2h | two thousand and fifth; Dative year expression after к; only the final ordinal declines and agrees with году. К marks the year by which construction was complete. |
| ru-04-0755 | B2h | nineteen ninetieth; Accusative masculine singular year expression after за; the final ordinal agrees with год. За identifies the year covered by the surviving data. |
| ru-04-0756 | B2h | the first and second; Instrumental masculine singular ordinals after между; этапами is instrumental plural. Между identifies the two stages that bound the interval. |
| ru-04-0757 | B2h | every third; Nominative masculine singular; both words agree with автобус. Каждый третий expresses a recurring proportion of one in three. |
| ru-04-0758 | B2h | nearly two thousand; The nominative quantity две тысячи has feminine две and genitive singular тысячи; экспонатов is genitive plural. Малого is genitive inside the fixed modifier and does not govern the numeral or экспонатов. Без малого indicates a little less than the stated number. |
| ru-04-0759 | B2h | one hundred and fifty; Genitive numeral after не менее; комплектов is genitive plural. Не менее sets a lower bound that includes the stated quantity. |
| ru-04-0760 | B2h | two out of five; Две is nominative feminine; пяти is genitive after из, as is семей. Из identifies the total set against which the number of families planning a move is compared. |
| ru-04-0761 | B2h | three hundred and twenty; Inanimate accusative compound numeral after на; рублей is genitive plural. Отличается на gives the absolute difference between the estimates. |
| ru-04-0762 | B2h | two hundred; Genitive numeral after без; рублей is also genitive plural. Без marks the sum whose absence prevents payment. |
| ru-04-0763 | B2h | four hundred; Dative numeral after к; рублям is also dative plural. Прибавить к identifies the amount to which the delivery cost was added. |
| ru-04-0764 | B2h | several hundred; Instrumental plural quantity phrase governed by управлять; устройств is genitive plural. Управлять names the devices controlled through the application. |
| ru-04-0765 | B2h | one thousand; Genitive singular after достижении; заявок is genitive plural. Достижение names the threshold at which applications stop. |
| ru-04-0766 | B2h | one and a half thousand; Prepositional quantity phrase after о; выпускников is genitive plural. Сведения о identifies the group described in the report. |
| ru-04-0767 | B2h | fifty; Instrumental numeral governed by ограничились; both components inflect, and анкетами is instrumental plural. |
| ru-04-0768 | B2h | one hundred; Genitive numeral after из; каждых and отправленных писем are also genitive plural. Из каждых ста expresses a recurring rate: five returns per hundred messages. |
| ru-04-0769 | B2h | fifty-fifty; Fixed comparative expression with two cardinal numeral forms. Пятьдесят на пятьдесят describes the teams' chances as equal. |
| ru-04-0770 | B2h | two point three; Nominative decimal quantity phrase; вопроса is genitive singular. Приходится на expresses an average per participant, not a count for each individual. |
| ru-04-0771 | B2h | one tenth; Accusative feminine singular fraction after на; грамма is genitive singular. Отличается на gives the size of the difference from the standard. |
| ru-04-0772 | B2h | one tenth; Genitive feminine singular fraction after менее; объёма is also genitive singular. Менее sets an upper bound on the remaining share. |
| ru-04-0773 | B2h | two fifths; Dative fraction after к; суммы is genitive singular. Добавить к identifies the share to which the ticket revenue was added. |
| ru-04-0774 | B2h | one quarter; Instrumental feminine singular fraction governed by ограничились; списка is genitive singular. Ограничиться names the share that was considered sufficient. |
| ru-04-0775 | B2h | one third; Instrumental feminine singular fraction after между; бюджета is genitive singular. Между introduces the two shares being compared. |
| ru-04-0776 | B2h | three quarters; Inanimate accusative fraction; стакана is genitive singular. The fraction measures the amount of water against the capacity of a glass. |
| ru-04-0777 | B2h | one hundredth; Genitive feminine singular fraction after до; процента is genitive singular. С точностью до states the precision of the reading. |
| ru-04-0778 | B2h | two percent; Genitive quantity phrase after менее; бюджета is genitive singular. Менее sets an upper bound on the share of the budget. |
| ru-04-0779 | B2h | five percent; Dative quantity phrase after к; скидки is genitive singular. Добавляется к presents free delivery as an additional benefit alongside the discount. |
| ru-04-0780 | B2h | seventy percent; Instrumental quantity phrase governed by доволен; выполнения is genitive singular after процентами, and плана is genitive singular dependent on выполнения. Доволен identifies the extent of completion that satisfied the director. |
| ru-04-0781 | B2h | minus twenty-five; Двадцати пяти is genitive after до; минус is invariable. Минус marks the temperature as negative; до gives the temperature reached. |
| ru-04-0782 | B2h | eighty percent; Inanimate accusative quantity phrase after за; цены is genitive singular. За states the sale price as a proportion of the original price. |
| ru-04-0783 | B2h | at most ten; Силы is genitive singular inside the fixed modifier; десять is nominative in the quantity subject, with минут in the genitive plural. От силы gives an approximate upper estimate of the time available for packing. |
| ru-04-0784 | B2h | six people; Nominative collective numeral; студентов is genitive plural. Шестеро can refer to men or a mixed group, but not to a group consisting only of women. |
| ru-04-0785 | B2h | just over two hours; The inanimate accusative duration quantity follows заняла; часа is genitive singular after два. Небольшим is instrumental neuter singular in the fixed phrase с небольшим. С небольшим adds a small amount to the stated duration. |
| ru-04-0786 | B2h | two parts; Inanimate accusative quantity phrase; части is genitive singular after две. На одну часть растворителя establishes a ratio of two parts paint to one part solvent. |
| ru-04-0787 | B2h | one and a half thousand; Nominative quantity phrase; тысячи is genitive singular after feminine полторы, and знаков is genitive plural. На одну страницу gives the unit for the average character count. |
| ru-04-0788 | B2h | five hundred kilobytes; Genitive quantity phrase after до; килобайт is genitive plural. The accepted килобайтов is also a standard genitive plural. С ... до gives the original and final file sizes. |
| ru-04-0789 | B2h | half; Accusative singular feminine after на; поездки is genitive singular. Хватило менее чем на expresses the duration for which the charge was sufficient. |
| ru-04-0790 | B2h | three quarters; Dative fraction after к; суммы is genitive singular. Приблизиться к identifies the share of the planned amount nearly reached. |
| ru-04-0791 | B2h | one and a half times; Inanimate accusative quantity phrase after в; раза is genitive singular. Выросла в полтора раза means that revenue reached one and a half times its January level. |
| ru-04-0792 | B2h | three times; Втрое дольше means three times as long; старой identifies the battery used for comparison. |
| ru-04-0793 | B2h | twenty-seventh; Genitive masculine singular compound ordinal in a written year; only седьмого declines and agrees with года. С marks the start date on which the tariff takes effect. |
| ru-04-0794 | B2h | two thousandth; Dative masculine singular ordinal after к, agreeing with году. This single-word ordinal itself declines. К gives the year by which the museum completed the conversion. |
| ru-04-0795 | B2h | the twenty-second; Instrumental neuter singular compound date ordinal after между; only вторым declines, and мая remains genitive. Между identifies the dates bounding the closure. |
| ru-04-0796 | B2h | the twenty-ninth; Nominative neuter singular compound date ordinal; февраля is genitive singular. The date is the item incorrectly stated in the schedule. |
| ru-04-0797 | B2h | ten minutes; Inanimate accusative quantity phrase after за; минут is genitive plural. За ... до specifies how long before midnight the bus departed. |
| ru-04-0798 | B2h | two and a half; Inanimate accusative quantity phrase after спустя; часа is genitive singular. Спустя gives the elapsed interval after departure. |
| ru-04-0799 | B2h | twenty-one point five; Genitive decimal numeral after до; градуса remains genitive singular. До gives the temperature reached by the water. |
| ru-04-0800 | B2h | two grams; Genitive quantity phrase after не больше; соли is genitive singular. Не больше sets an upper limit on the amount of salt per ten litres of water. |
| ru-04-0801 | B2i | signing; Nominative singular neuter; subject of назначено. Договора names what will be signed; директором names the person who will sign it. |
| ru-04-0802 | B2i | examination; Genitive singular masculine after после. Врачом identifies the examiner; пациент is the person examined. |
| ru-04-0803 | B2i | publication; Nominative singular feminine; subject of поможет. Результатов names what will be published. |
| ru-04-0804 | B2i | participation; Genitive singular neuter after для. Участие в конкурсе names the activity for which the form is needed. |
| ru-04-0805 | B2i | performance; Nominative singular neuter; subject of началось. Пианистки identifies the performer, not the person receiving the performance. |
| ru-04-0806 | B2i | construction; Nominative singular neuter; subject of завершится. Моста names what is being built; через реку describes the bridge. |
| ru-04-0807 | B2i | discussion; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of перенести. Бюджета names the subject to be discussed. |
| ru-04-0808 | B2i | handover; Nominative singular feminine; subject of состоится. Ключей names what will be handed over; the dative жильцам identifies the recipients. |
| ru-04-0809 | B2i | request; Instrumental singular feminine after с. Просьба об отсрочке identifies what the letter asks for. |
| ru-04-0810 | B2i | departure; Genitive singular masculine after после. Гостей identifies the people who departed. |
| ru-04-0811 | B2i | opening; Nominative singular neuter; subject of запланировано. Выставки names what will be opened; художницей identifies the person who will open it. |
| ru-04-0812 | B2i | check; Nominative singular feminine; subject of заняла. Билетов names what was checked; контролёром identifies the person who checked them. |
| ru-04-0813 | B2i | invitation; Dative singular neuter after обрадовались. The relative clause которое получила их дочь explicitly identifies the daughter as the recipient; на стажировку states what she was invited to. |
| ru-04-0814 | B2i | processing; Accusative singular feminine after на. Заявки names what is processed; на introduces the task requiring time. |
| ru-04-0815 | B2i | apology; Nominative singular neuter; subject of помогло. Соседа identifies the person who apologized. |
| ru-04-0816 | B2i | storage; Genitive singular neuter after для. Овощей names what will be stored. |
| ru-04-0817 | B2i | appointment; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of объявили. Марии identifies the person appointed; руководителем группы gives her new role. |
| ru-04-0818 | B2i | description; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of сократить. Главного героя identifies the person described. |
| ru-04-0819 | B2i | provision; Accusative singular neuter after на, which marks the purpose of the funding. Обеспечение кого чем names the recipients in the genitive and the goods provided in the instrumental: школ and новыми компьютерами. The formal frame is common in written funding contexts. |
| ru-04-0820 | B2i | changes; Accusative plural neuter; direct object of внести. Внести изменения в проект identifies the thing to be changed with в and the accusative. |
| ru-04-0821 | B2i | training; Nominative singular neuter; subject of займёт. Сотрудников identifies the people being trained; работе с программой names the skill taught. |
| ru-04-0822 | B2i | criticism; Nominative singular feminine; subject of помогла. Статьи names what was criticized; рецензентом identifies the critic. |
| ru-04-0823 | B2i | planting; Accusative singular feminine after на. Деревьев names what the neighbours gathered to plant. |
| ru-04-0824 | B2i | collection; Nominative singular neuter; subject of возможно. Посылки names what is collected; при предъявлении паспорта states the condition. |
| ru-04-0825 | B2i | refusal; Nominative singular masculine; subject of не остановил. Банка identifies the party refusing; в кредите names what was refused. |
| ru-04-0826 | B2i | reduction; Genitive singular neuter after причину. Расходов names what was reduced; на рекламу specifies the expenditure. |
| ru-04-0827 | B2i | appeal; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of опубликовали. Жителей identifies the authors of the appeal; к мэру identifies its addressee. |
| ru-04-0828 | B2i | comparison; Genitive singular neuter after для. Цен names what is to be compared. |
| ru-04-0829 | B2i | communication; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of поручили. Пациенту identifies the recipient; результатов анализа names the information to be communicated. |
| ru-04-0830 | B2i | switch; Nominative singular masculine; subject of произойдёт. Переход на новый тариф names the change to a new plan. |
| ru-04-0831 | B2i | return; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of откладывать. Книги names what must be returned; в библиотеку gives its destination. |
| ru-04-0832 | B2i | approval; Nominative singular neuter; subject of позволит. Проекта names what requires approval; комиссией identifies the body that may approve it. |
| ru-04-0833 | B2i | help; Accusative singular feminine after за. За помощь states the reason for thanks; пожилым соседям identifies the recipients of the help. |
| ru-04-0834 | B2i | leak; Nominative singular feminine; subject of повредила. Воды names what leaked; из трубы identifies its source. |
| ru-04-0835 | B2i | delivery; Accusative singular feminine after на. Расходы на доставку names the cost of delivery; мебели identifies what is delivered. |
| ru-04-0836 | B2i | acknowledgment; Nominative singular neuter; subject of помогло. Ошибки names what was acknowledged; руководителем identifies the person who acknowledged it. |
| ru-04-0837 | B2i | replacement; Genitive singular feminine after после. Батареек names the items that were replaced. |
| ru-04-0838 | B2i | collection; Nominative singular masculine; subject of продолжится. Подписей names what is being collected; под петицией identifies the petition being signed. |
| ru-04-0839 | B2i | cancellation; Prepositional singular feminine after об. Уведомление об отмене reports a cancellation; занятия names what was cancelled. |
| ru-04-0840 | B2i | preparation; Nominative singular neuter; subject of требует. Теста names what is prepared; вручную specifies the method. |
| ru-04-0841 | B2i | delivery; Nominative singular feminine; subject of организована. Продуктов names the goods delivered, пожилым людям the recipients, and местными волонтёрами the organizers. |
| ru-04-0842 | B2i | consideration; Nominative singular neuter; subject of займёт. Жалобы names what will be considered; комиссией identifies the body that will consider it. |
| ru-04-0843 | B2i | intervention; Genitive singular neuter after после. Соседки identifies the person who intervened. |
| ru-04-0844 | B2i | processing; Genitive singular neuter after для. Визы names the document being arranged; для marks the purpose of attaching the photograph. |
| ru-04-0845 | B2i | meeting; Nominative singular feminine; subject of помогла. С переводчицей identifies the person the author met. |
| ru-04-0846 | B2i | preparation; Accusative singular feminine; direct object of поручил. Вопросов names what Anna was asked to prepare; для интервью states their purpose. |
| ru-04-0847 | B2i | proposal; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of поддержали. Учёных identifies the people who made the proposal; о создании заповедника states its content. |
| ru-04-0848 | B2i | drop; Nominative singular neuter; subject of спасло. Температуры names what fell; the noun describes a change without identifying an agent. |
| ru-04-0849 | B2i | restoration; Accusative singular neuter after на. Старой фотографии names what the craftsperson restored; на marks the activity that took time. |
| ru-04-0850 | B2i | explanation; Nominative singular neuter; subject of убедило. Преподавателя identifies the person who gave the explanation. |
| ru-04-0851 | B2i | sending; Genitive singular feminine after для. Документов names what will be sent; для identifies the purpose of the address. |
| ru-04-0852 | B2i | move; Genitive singular masculine after после. Дочери identifies the person who moved; ей refers back to her. |
| ru-04-0853 | B2i | payment; Nominative singular feminine; subject of предусмотрена. Курсов names what is paid for; работодателем identifies the payer. |
| ru-04-0854 | B2i | request; Accusative singular feminine; direct object of выполнили. Соседа identifies the requester; выключить музыку states what he asked us to do. |
| ru-04-0855 | B2i | issuing; Nominative singular feminine; subject of начнётся. Справок names the documents issued; студентам identifies their recipients. |
| ru-04-0856 | B2i | disappearance; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of заметили. Птиц identifies what disappeared; из сада indicates where they disappeared from. |
| ru-04-0857 | B2i | translation; Accusative singular masculine after за. Романа names the work translated; на русский язык identifies the target language. |
| ru-04-0858 | B2i | reply; Nominative singular masculine; subject of пришёл. Организаторов identifies the authors of the reply; на жалобу names what they replied to. |
| ru-04-0859 | B2i | access; Genitive singular masculine after для. Доступ к архиву names the access sought; к takes the dative. |
| ru-04-0860 | B2i | departure; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of задержали. Поезда identifies the train that was due to depart. |
| ru-04-0861 | B2i | care; Accusative singular masculine; direct object of доверили. Уход за собакой means caring for the dog; за takes the instrumental. |
| ru-04-0862 | B2i | reimbursement; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of выплатила. Компания identifies the payer, Ирине the recipient, and расходов на проезд the travel costs reimbursed. |
| ru-04-0863 | B2i | support; Accusative singular feminine; direct object of оказал. Оказать поддержку кому uses the dative for the recipients; молодым музыкантам identifies them, and фонд is the provider. |
| ru-04-0864 | B2i | introduction; Genitive singular neuter dependent on дату. Новых правил names what is introduced; дату введения identifies the date of their introduction. |
| ru-04-0865 | B2i | move; Nominative singular masculine; subject of потребовал. В другой город names the destination of the move; от семьи identifies who bore the cost. |
| ru-04-0866 | B2i | enrollment; Nominative singular feminine; subject of возможна. Ребёнка identifies the person to be enrolled; в кружок names the activity. |
| ru-04-0867 | B2i | arrival; Nominative singular masculine; subject of обрадовал. Сестры identifies the person who arrived; на выходные gives the intended duration of her visit. |
| ru-04-0868 | B2i | closure; Prepositional singular neuter after о. Дороги names what is closed; сообщили о introduces the event reported. |
| ru-04-0869 | B2i | removal; Genitive singular neuter after после. Пятна names what is removed from the fabric. |
| ru-04-0870 | B2i | warning; Nominative singular neuter; subject of помогло. Врача identifies the person who gave the warning; о побочных эффектах names its subject. |
| ru-04-0871 | B2i | treatment; Accusative singular neuter after на. Раненой птицы identifies the animal to be treated; на marks the purpose of the collection. |
| ru-04-0872 | B2i | opposition; Nominative singular neuter; subject of объединило. Выступление против expresses public opposition; строительства дороги names what is opposed. |
| ru-04-0873 | B2i | exchange; Nominative singular masculine; subject of помог. Книгами names the items exchanged; с соседями identifies the other participants. |
| ru-04-0874 | B2i | registration; Genitive singular feminine after для. Регистрация на конференцию names enrollment for the event; для marks the purpose of filling in the form. |
| ru-04-0875 | B2i | cutoff; Nominative singular neuter; subject of прервало. Электричества names the supply that was cut off; no agent is specified. |
| ru-04-0876 | B2i | installation; Prepositional singular feminine after об. Попросить об установке states the requested action; полки names what is to be installed. |
| ru-04-0877 | B2i | visit; Nominative singular neuter; subject of запланировано. Музея identifies the place to be visited; школьниками identifies the visitors. |
| ru-04-0878 | B2i | cancellation; Accusative singular masculine; direct object of оформить. Пассажира identifies the person cancelling; отказ от поездки names the trip they are giving up. |

| ru-04-1157 | B2l | entry; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of изучила. Запись в карте is an individual written entry in a record. |
| ru-04-1158 | B2l | spelling; Accusative singular neuter inanimate; object of проверить. Правописание сложных слов concerns their conventional spelling, rather than the appearance of someone's handwriting. |
| ru-04-1159 | B2l | handwritten; Accusative singular feminine adjective, agreeing with страницу, the object of показали. Рукописную describes a page written by hand, contrasted with its printed copy. |
| ru-04-1160 | B2l | literary writing; Instrumental singular neuter, governed by занялась. Писательство is a colloquial term for literary writing as an activity; рассказы identifies the creative work. |
| ru-04-1161 | B2l | census; Genitive singular feminine after во время. Перепись is an organized count and collection of information about a population. |
| ru-04-1162 | B2l | subscriber; Nominative singular masculine animate; subject of может предложить. Подписчик канала is a person subscribed to the channel. |
| ru-04-1163 | B2l | postscript; Nominative singular feminine inanimate; subject of была. Приписка is an additional passage appended to an existing piece of writing. |
| ru-04-1164 | B2l | obvious and familiar; Nominative singular feminine, agreeing with истина. Прописная истина is a familiar, obvious truth; the phrase can suggest that repeating it is unnecessary. |
| ru-04-1165 | B2l | handwriting practice books; Prepositional plural after в; прописи is used in the plural in this sense. Прописи are practice books used to learn how to form letters by hand. |
| ru-04-1166 | B2l | determined in advance; Masculine singular past, agreeing with гол. Perfective предрешить; imperfective предрешать. Future form предреши́т. It takes an accusative object; the past presents the outcome as determined by the early goal. Предрешить исход means to determine an outcome before the event is over. |
| ru-04-1167 | B2l | indecisive; Nominative singular masculine, agreeing with покупатель. Нерешительный describes someone prone to hesitation when making a choice. |
| ru-04-1168 | B2l | copied out; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Маша. Perfective выписать; imperfective выписывать. Future form вы́пишет. It takes an accusative object and из plus the genitive for the source; the past reports completed extraction. Выписать термины из статьи means to copy selected terms out of an article. |
| ru-04-1169 | B2l | subscribes to; Third-person singular present, agreeing with дедушка. Imperfective выписывать; выписать can refer to arranging a subscription for a specified period. Compare вы́писываю. It takes an accusative publication; the present describes an ongoing subscription. Выписывать газету means to subscribe to a newspaper for regular delivery. |
| ru-04-1170 | B2l | added in writing; Feminine singular past, agreeing with сотрудница. Perfective дописать; imperfective дописывать. Future form допи́шет. It takes an accusative object; the past reports a completed addition. Дописать дату means to add a missing date to existing writing. |
| ru-04-1171 | B2l | attribute; Second-person singular imperative, negated by не. Imperfective списывать; perfective списать. Present form спи́сывает. It takes an accusative object and на plus the accusative for the supposed cause; the negative imperative addresses a repeated habit. Списывать что-то на что-то means to explain or excuse something by attributing it to a cause. |
| ru-04-1172 | B2l | got in touch in writing; Plural past reflexive form, agreeing with мы. Perfective списаться; imperfective списываться. Future form спи́шется. С plus the instrumental identifies the other person; the past presents the contact as established. Списаться с организатором в чате means to get in touch with the organizer by exchanging messages. |
| ru-04-1173 | B2l | scheduled; Feminine singular past, agreeing with она. Perfective расписать; imperfective расписывать. Future form распи́шет. It takes an accusative object; the past presents the scheduling as complete. Расписать встречи по часам means to assign the meetings to specific times. |
| ru-04-1174 | B2l | described vividly; Feminine singular past, agreeing with она. Perfective расписать; imperfective расписывать. Future form распи́шет. It takes an accusative object; the past reports a completed description. Расписать can mean to describe something at length and vividly, often emphasizing its attractions. |
| ru-04-1175 | B2l | fit through; Masculine singular past reflexive form, matching visible задел for the same driver. Perfective вписаться; imperfective вписываться. Future form впи́шется. It takes в plus the accusative; the negative past reports a failed maneuver. Не вписаться в ворота means to fail to maneuver a vehicle through the opening without hitting something. |
| ru-04-1176 | B2l | fitted in; Feminine singular past reflexive form, agreeing with новенькая. Perfective вписаться; imperfective вписываться. Future form впи́шется. It takes в plus the accusative; the past presents successful integration as complete. Вписаться в команду means to fit in socially and function well within a team. |
| ru-04-1177 | B2l | correspondence; Accusative singular feminine inanimate, object of завязали. Завязать переписку means to begin exchanging letters or messages with someone. |
| ru-04-1178 | B2l | descriptive; Instrumental singular masculine adjective, agreeing with доклад after назвал. Описательный characterizes the report's presentation as listing facts rather than analyzing them. |
| ru-04-1179 | B2l | indescribable; Accusative singular neuter adjective, agreeing with облегчение, the object of испытали. Неописуемое облегчение emphasizes the intensity of the relief. |
| ru-04-1180 | B2l | put names on; Perfective infinitive governed by попросила. Perfective подписать; imperfective подписывать. Future form подпи́шет. It takes an accusative object; the request calls for the names to be added. Подписать тетради means to write identifying names on the notebooks, not to sign a document. |
| ru-04-1181 | B2l | sign; Perfective reflexive infinitive governed by попросили. Perfective расписаться; imperfective расписываться. Future form распи́шется. За plus the accusative identifies what the signature acknowledges; the request calls for a completed signature. Расписаться за книги means to sign to acknowledge receipt of the books. |
| ru-04-1182 | B2l | registered their marriage; Plural past reflexive form, agreeing with они. Perfective расписаться; imperfective расписываться. Future form распи́шутся. A plural subject names the couple; the past presents registration as complete. Расписаться в загсе colloquially means to register a marriage at a civil registry office. |
| ru-04-1183 | B2l | decoratively painted; Accusative singular feminine adjective, agreeing with шкатулку, the object of купила. Расписная шкатулка is a box decorated with painted designs; the sentence specifies a floral pattern. |
| ru-04-1184 | B2l | set out in writing; Plural past with an unspecified human subject. Perfective прописать; imperfective прописывать. Future form пропи́шет. It takes an accusative object and в plus the prepositional for the document; the past presents the written provision as complete. Прописать порядок оплаты в договоре means to specify the payment procedure in the contract. |
| ru-04-1185 | B2l | debit; Accusative singular neuter inanimate; object of обнаружила. Списание is a debit recorded against an account; дублирующее identifies it as a duplicate charge. |
| ru-04-1186 | B2l | is decisive; Third-person singular present; опыт is the factor identified as decisive. Imperfective решать; решить can describe determining a particular outcome. Compare реша́ют. Here it has no explicit object and the present states a general assessment. Решает identifies the factor that determines the outcome; не возраст, а опыт makes the contrast explicit. |
| ru-04-1187 | B2l | settle; First-person plural simple future. Perfective решить; imperfective решать. Compare реши́т. It takes an accusative object; the future presents settling the matter as a completed result. Решить вопрос о дате means to settle the matter of the date. |
| ru-04-1188 | B2l | writing; Accusative singular neuter inanimate after на. На написание отчёта ушло два часа states how much time composing the report took. |
| ru-04-1189 | B2l | fully endorse; First-person singular present reflexive form. Imperfective подписываться; perfective подписаться can express a particular act of endorsement. Present form подпи́сывается. Под plus the instrumental identifies the statement endorsed; the present expresses the speaker's agreement now. Подписываться под каждым словом figuratively means to endorse every word. |
| ru-04-1190 | B2l | signatories; Genitive plural masculine animate, dependent on список. Подписанты are people who signed a collective petition. General dictionaries mark this sense as colloquial; it differs from подписчики, subscribers. |
| ru-04-1191 | B2l | woman writer; Nominative singular feminine animate; subject of прочитала. Писательница names a woman who writes literary works. |
| ru-04-1192 | B2l | of a writer; Nominative singular masculine, agreeing with труд. Писательский труд refers to the work involved in writing literary texts. |
| ru-04-1193 | B2l | listed; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Игорь. Perfective переписать; imperfective переписывать. Future form перепи́шет. It takes an animate accusative group; the past presents the enumeration as complete. Переписать всех участников means to put all the participants on a list; по фамилиям specifies how they were identified. |
| ru-04-1194 | B2l | transferred ownership; Feminine singular past, agreeing with бабушка. Perfective переписать; imperfective переписывать. Future form перепи́шет. It takes an accusative property and на plus the accusative for its new owner; the past reports a completed transfer. Переписать дом на внучку colloquially describes transferring ownership of the house to her; the source reports this after gift documentation was completed. |
| ru-04-1195 | B2l | inveterate; Nominative singular masculine adjective, agreeing with спорщик. Записной спорщик is a habitual arguer; the frequent objections make the colloquial characterization clear. |
| ru-04-1196 | B2l | fictitious additions; Accusative plural feminine inanimate; object of выявила. Приписки are fictitious additions that inflate reported figures; the report states 40 participants when only 20 actually participated. |

The instrumental names the form of the signature; the past reports completed signing. Подписаться именем means to sign using that name; here the writer omitted her surname. |
| ru-04-1198 | B2l | writing system; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of придумала. Письменность is a system of written signs used to represent language. |
| ru-04-1199 | B2l | video recording; Prepositional singular feminine after на. На видеозаписи identifies the recording in which the speaker's gestures are visible. |
| ru-04-1200 | B2l | overwriting; Nominative singular feminine inanimate; subject of заменила. Перезапись файла here means overwriting a digital file so that the new version replaces the old one. |
| ru-04-1201 | B2m | repetitions; Accusative plural neuter inanimate; object of убрать. Повторения are repeated words or ideas in the text. |
| ru-04-1202 | B2m | reworked; Masculine singular past; accepted feminine переработала is also possible because автор does not establish the person's gender. Perfective; imperfective перерабатывать. Future forms include переработаю and переработает. It takes an accusative object; the past presents the revision as completed. Переработать вступление means to revise the introduction substantially. |
| ru-04-1203 | B2m | presentation; Nominative singular neuter inanimate; subject of получилось. Изложение refers to the way the material is presented, not to a school summary exercise. |

It takes an accusative object; the past reports a completed simplification. Упростить вопрос means to present the issue in simpler terms, potentially leaving out complexity. |
| ru-04-1205 | B2m | misleading; Instrumental singular masculine, agreeing with заголовок after оказался. Обманчивый describes an impression that leads readers to an incorrect expectation; it does not necessarily imply deliberate deception. |
| ru-04-1206 | B2m | supported; Masculine singular past; accepted feminine подкрепила is also possible because автор does not establish the person's gender. Perfective; imperfective подкреплять. Ссылками names the evidence used to support the conclusion. |
| ru-04-1207 | B2m | biased; Instrumental singular masculine, agreeing with отзыв after оказался. Предвзятый describes a judgment shaped by a prior attitude; the author decided to criticize the book before reading it. |

| ru-04-1209 | B2m | being well informed; Instrumental singular feminine inanimate after удивила. Осведомлённость в вопросах экологии is informed knowledge about environmental issues. |

| ru-04-1211 | B2m | improvement; Accusative singular neuter inanimate; object of обсудили. Благоустройство двора means making the courtyard more usable and pleasant. |

| ru-04-1213 | B2m | resourcefulness; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of оценили. Находчивость is the ability to find a solution quickly in a difficult situation. |
| ru-04-1214 | B2m | judicious; Nominative singular masculine, describing руководитель. Рассудительный describes a person inclined to weigh matters carefully. |
| ru-04-1215 | B2m | restraint; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of проявила. Проявить сдержанность means to show restraint in one's reaction. |

| ru-04-1218 | B2m | tactfully; Тактично describes a reminder delivered with sensitivity to the other person's feelings. |

The duties previously performed by Olga alone are reassigned among three staff members. |
| ru-04-1221 | B2m | a misunderstanding; Genitive singular neuter inanimate after из-за. Недоразумение is a misunderstanding or mix-up. |

| ru-04-1224 | B2m | convincing; Instrumental singular neuter, agreeing with предложение after показалось. Убедительный describes an argument or proposal that gives good reason to accept it. |
| ru-04-1225 | B2m | a contradiction; Nominative singular neuter inanimate; subject of обнаружилось. Между with the instrumental identifies the two aspects that conflict. |

Present forms include отношусь and относятся. Относиться к чему takes the dative and expresses a continuing relation. Не относится к теме means that the remark is not relevant to the topic. |
| ru-04-1227 | B2m | compatibility; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of проверьте. Совместимость чего с чем names the two items that must work together; с takes the instrumental. |

The settings are the subject; the past reports their completed return to default values. Настройки сбросились describes a reset without identifying an agent. |
| ru-04-1229 | B2m | a malfunction; Prepositional singular feminine inanimate after о. Неполадка is a fault or disruption in the operation of a system or process. |
| ru-04-1230 | B2m | carry out; Perfective infinitive after удалось. Perfective; imperfective осуществлять. Future form осуществит. It takes an accusative object; the infinitive with удалось presents successful completion. Осуществить проект is a formal way to say that a project was carried out. |
| ru-04-1231 | B2m | a hitch; Nominative singular feminine inanimate; subject of возникла. Заминка is a temporary hitch or delay; it is a conversational choice for the missing key's effect. |

| ru-04-1233 | B2m | entrusted; Masculine singular past; accepted feminine поручила is also possible because директор does not establish the person's gender. Поручить Олегу подготовку assigns Oleg responsibility for the preparation. |
| ru-04-1234 | B2m | devalued; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Игорь. Perfective; imperfective обесценивать. Future form обесце́нит. | ru-04-1242 | B2m | helpful; Nominative singular masculine, agreeing with сосед. Отзывчивый describes someone willing to respond to others' needs. |
| ru-04-1243 | B2m | indifference; Nominative singular neuter inanimate; subject of удивило. Равнодушие к чему takes the dative and names a lack of concern about something. |
| ru-04-1244 | B2m | mediocre; Instrumental singular masculine, agreeing with сценарий after оказался. Посредственный evaluates the script as unremarkable in quality. |
| ru-04-1245 | B2m | direct; Instrumental singular masculine, agreeing with начальником after с. Непосредственный начальник is the person to whom an employee reports directly. |
| ru-04-1246 | B2m | reflection; Accusative singular neuter inanimate after на. Осмысление увиденного means the process of making sense of what one has seen. |
| ru-04-1247 | B2m | well-founded; Nominative plural, agreeing with опасения. Обоснованные опасения are concerns supported by reasons; the construction nearby provides their basis. |
| ru-04-1248 | B2m | prejudice; Genitive singular neuter inanimate after от. Предубеждение против кого names a preconceived negative attitude toward people. |
| ru-04-1249 | B2m | overconfidence; Nominative singular feminine inanimate; subject of помешала. Самоуверенность suggests excessive confidence and often carries a negative evaluation. |
| ru-04-1250 | B2m | sensitive; Short-form plural adjective, agreeing with дети. Восприимчивый к чему takes the dative and here means sensitive to an emotional influence. |
| ru-04-1251 | B2m | inaction; Nominative singular neuter inanimate; subject of вызвало. Бездействие руководства presents management's failure to act as the cause of dissatisfaction. |
| ru-04-1252 | B2m | concern; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of выразили. Обеспокоенность чем takes the instrumental to name the cause of concern. |
| ru-04-1253 | B2m | with care; Бережно describes care taken to protect the photographs from damage. |
| ru-04-1254 | B2m | carelessness; Nominative singular feminine inanimate; subject of затруднила. Небрежность в оформлении names a lack of care in preparing the application. |
| ru-04-1255 | B2m | clarify; Perfective infinitive after удалось. Perfective; imperfective прояснять. Future forms include проясню́ and проясни́т. It takes an accusative object; удалось presents the clarification as successful. Прояснить причину means to make the reason clear. |
| ru-04-1256 | B2m | impression; Accusative singular neuter inanimate; object of произвёл. Произвести впечатление на кого means to make an impression on someone; на takes the accusative. |
| ru-04-1257 | B2m | well-being; Prepositional singular neuter inanimate after о. Самочувствие refers to a person's physical or general state as they experience it. |

| ru-04-1259 | B2m | goodwill; Accusative singular feminine inanimate after за. Доброжелательность is a kindly, well-disposed attitude toward others. |
| ru-04-1260 | B2m | unfriendly; Недоброжелательно describes the unfriendly impression made by the remark. |
| ru-04-1261 | B2m | took heed; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with организаторы. Perfective; imperfective прислушиваться. Future forms include прислу́шаюсь and прислу́шается. К with the dative names the views considered; the past reports a completed response. Прислушаться к замечаниям means to take comments into consideration. |
| ru-04-1262 | B2m | appropriate; Instrumental singular neuter, agreeing with замечание after было. Уместный describes a remark suited to the situation. |

| ru-04-1265 | B2m | confiding; Instrumental singular masculine, agreeing with разговор after получился. Доверительный describes an open conversation marked by mutual confidence; доверчивый describes a person inclined to believe others readily. |
| ru-04-1266 | B2m | inaccuracy; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of признала. Неточность here is a specific inaccurate detail, namely the date. |
| ru-04-1267 | B2m | reliably; Достоверно установить means to establish reliably from evidence. |
| ru-04-1268 | B2m | response; Accusative singular masculine inanimate; object of вызвала. Общественный резонанс is a notable response among the public, not physical resonance. |
| ru-04-1269 | B2m | touched on; Feminine singular past; accepted masculine затронул is also possible because профессор does not establish the person's gender. Perfective; imperfective затрагивать. | ru-04-1270 | B2m | initiative; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of проявили. Проявить инициативу means to act independently to get something started. |
| ru-04-1271 | B2m | in coordination; Слаженно describes people acting together in a coordinated way. |

| ru-04-1277 | B2m | authenticity; Prepositional singular feminine inanimate after в. Подлинность картины concerns whether the painting is genuine. |
| ru-04-1278 | B2m | by accident; Нечаянно is a conversational way to say that the deletion was unintentional. |
| ru-04-1279 | B2m | deliberately; Намеренно qualifies the decision not to name the complainant as deliberate. |

| ru-04-1286 | B2m | curiosity; Instrumental singular neuter inanimate after с. С любопытством describes the interested manner in which the children watched. |
| ru-04-1287 | B2m | durability; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of отметил. Долговечность мебели refers to its capacity to last a long time. |

| ru-04-1289 | B2m | confidentiality; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of соблюдать. Соблюдать конфиденциальность means to keep the personal stories private. |
| ru-04-1290 | B2m | inattention; Genitive singular feminine inanimate after из-за. Невнимательность names a lack of attention that caused the mistake. |
| ru-04-1291 | B2m | earned; Feminine singular past, agreeing with сотрудница. Perfective; imperfective завоёвывать. Future forms include завоюю and завоюет. It takes an accusative object; the past reports the trust gained. Завоевать доверие figuratively means to earn someone's trust. |
| ru-04-1292 | B2m | categorical; Nominative singular neuter, agreeing with заявление. Категоричное заявление expresses a position without qualification or room for doubt. |
| ru-04-1293 | B2m | reimburse; Perfective infinitive after согласился. Perfective; imperfective возмещать. Future forms include возмещу and возместит. It takes an accusative object; the infinitive names the reimbursement agreed to. Возместить расходы means to reimburse expenses. |
| ru-04-1294 | B2m | comparison; Nominative singular neuter inanimate; subject of выявило. Сопоставление двух отчётов is a formal way to describe comparing the reports side by side. |
| ru-04-1295 | B2m | perseverance; Nominative singular feminine inanimate; subject of требуется. Усидчивость is the ability to work patiently at a task for a long time. |
| ru-04-1296 | B2m | emphasized; Feminine singular past, agreeing with учёная. Perfective; imperfective подчёркивать. Future forms include подчеркну and подчеркнёт.
