| ru-04-1297 | B2m | independence; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of поощряют. Самостоятельность сына refers to his ability to act and choose independently. |
| ru-04-1298 | B2m | begin to doubt; Perfective reflexive infinitive after заставило. Perfective; imperfective сомневаться describes the state of doubt. Future forms include усомнюсь and усомнится. Усомниться в takes the prepositional; the infinitive describes doubt arising. Усомниться в версии means to begin to doubt an account. |
| ru-04-1299 | B2m | ambiguously; Двусмысленно describes wording open to more than one interpretation. |
| ru-04-1300 | B2m | a typo; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of исправила. Опечатка is an accidental error in typed or printed text. |
| ru-04-1301 | B2n | I would venture to object; Позволю is first-person singular future; the reflexive pronoun себе is dative and возразить is an infinitive. Позволить is perfective, with imperfective позволять; second conjugation: позво́лит. It takes dative себе and an infinitive. Возразить is perfective, with imperfective возражать; second conjugation: возрази́т. It can take a dative addressee. Here позволю performs a present objection rather than predicting a future action. The speaker softens an objection to colleagues' proposed launch and immediately gives the reason. |
| ru-04-1302 | B2n | with all due respect; При governs prepositional singular уважении; всём agrees with it. The phrase introduces direct disagreement while acknowledging the addressee respectfully. |
| ru-04-1303 | B2n | surely you agree; Second-person plural reflexive imperative, addressing the colleagues. Perfective; imperfective соглашаться. Future forms include соглашусь and согласится. Here a что-clause states the proposition; the imperative invites agreement. Согласитесь appeals to the listeners to accept the stated argument; it can sound assertive. |
| ru-04-1304 | B2n | it is possible; Negated short neuter passive participle used impersonally with a что-clause. Perfective исключить; imperfective исключать. Compare исключи́т. The impersonal negated short passive construction не исключено with a что-clause presents a possibility; it does not assert that an exclusion occurred in the past. Не исключено presents the increased workload as a possibility, not a certainty. |
| ru-04-1305 | B2n | refrain; First-person plural perfective future, reflexive, used with давайте as a proposal. Perfective; imperfective воздерживаться. Future forms include воздержусь and воздержимся. От governs the genitive of the action avoided; the proposal concerns a temporary restraint. Воздержаться от решения means to refrain from making a decision. |
| ru-04-1306 | B2n | provided; При governs prepositional singular условии. При условии, что introduces the condition on which the customer accepts the price. |
| ru-04-1307 | B2n | are prepared; Short-form plural adjective, agreeing with мы; followed by an infinitive. Готовы снизить states willingness subject to the stated condition; it does not itself conclude an agreement. |
| ru-04-1308 | B2n | take into account; Perfective infinitive after прошу. Perfective; imperfective учитывать. Future forms include учту and учтёт. It takes an accusative object or a что-clause; the infinitive names the requested consideration. Прошу учесть asks the addressee to factor the existing urgent tasks into a decision. |
| ru-04-1309 | B2n | gives pause; Third-person singular present, agreeing with стоимость. Imperfective; contextual perfective смутить describes a particular reaction. Present forms include смущаю and смущает. It takes an accusative person; the present expresses a current concern. Меня смущает стоимость politely identifies the cost as a reason for hesitation. |
| ru-04-1310 | B2n | except for; The compound preposition governs genitive срока. За исключением limits the positive assessment by identifying the deadline as the objection. |
| ru-04-1311 | B2n | resolve; Perfective infinitive after прошу. Perfective; imperfective решать. Future forms include решу and решит. It takes an accusative object; the request asks for the problem to be resolved today. Решить проблему is a direct request for a remedy without prescribing the exact action. |
| ru-04-1312 | B2n | return to the issue; Perfective reflexive infinitive; к governs dative singular вопросу. Perfective вернуться; imperfective возвращаться. Future forms include вернусь and вернёмся. The figurative frame вернуться к вопросу takes the dative and proposes resuming consideration later. The speaker proposes resuming discussion after the costs have been assessed. |
| ru-04-1313 | B2n | no earlier than; Negated invariable comparative; понедельника is the genitive time limit. Не раньше sets the earliest time for the speaker's commitment to prepare the report. |
| ru-04-1314 | B2n | suppose; First-person plural perfective future used parenthetically to introduce a supposition. Perfective допустить; imperfective допускать. Future forms include допущу and допустим. In this parenthetical use a following clause states a hypothetical assumption; it does not report an actual decision. Допустим temporarily accepts the proposal as a premise for testing its consequences. |
| ru-04-1315 | B2n | count on; Imperfective infinitive after можем. Imperfective; in this expectation sense it has no simple perfective partner. Present forms include рассчитываю and рассчитывают. Рассчитывать на takes the accusative; the question asks what remedy is available. Рассчитывать на решение asks what outcome the customers can reasonably expect. |
| ru-04-1316 | B2n | will support; First-person singular perfective future. Perfective; imperfective поддерживать. Future forms include поддержу and поддержит. It takes an accusative proposal; the future commitment depends on the stated condition. Поддержу предложение commits the speaker to support once the costs are agreed. |
| ru-04-1317 | B2n | I suggest refraining; Предлагаю is first-person singular present; воздержаться is a perfective reflexive infinitive. Предлагать is imperfective, with perfective предложить; first conjugation, present предлага́ет. Its present form makes the proposal and takes an infinitive. Воздержаться is perfective, with imperfective воздерживаться; second conjugation, future возде́ржится. It takes от with the genitive; the proposed restraint lasts until the checks are complete. The speaker makes a formal proposal to colleagues; от выводов names what they are asked to refrain from. |
| ru-04-1318 | B2n | could it be possible; Impersonal predicate with interrogative ли, followed by an infinitive. Нельзя ли makes an indirect request to the person addressed to arrange a different assignment. |
| ru-04-1319 | B2n | I acknowledge; First-person singular present. Imperfective; perfective признать. Present forms include признаю and признаёт. It can take a что-clause; the present acknowledges the point in the current discussion. Признаю concedes a benefit before the speaker raises an objection. |
| ru-04-1320 | B2n | I will be able to confirm; Смогу is first-person singular perfective future; подтвердить is a perfective infinitive. Смочь is perfective, with contextual imperfective мочь; its conjugation is irregular, with future смо́жет. It takes an infinitive. Подтвердить is perfective, with imperfective подтверждать; second conjugation, future подтверди́т. It takes an accusative object; the future ability to confirm depends on the stated condition. The speaker tells the client that confirmation depends on colleagues approving the schedule. |
| ru-04-1321 | B2n | doubts; Nominative plural neuter, subject of есть. Сомнения относительно takes the genitive to name what is in doubt. |
| ru-04-1322 | B2n | premature; Instrumental singular masculine, agreeing with вывод after представляется. The speaker questions the timing of the conclusion rather than declaring it false. |
| ru-04-1323 | B2n | share; First-person singular present. Imperfective; perfective разделить. Present forms include разделяю and разделяет. It takes an accusative feeling or concern and expresses the speaker's current attitude. Разделять озабоченность means to share someone's concern. |
| ru-04-1324 | B2n | do not guarantee; Negated first-person plural present. Гарантировать can be imperfective or perfective; here it is imperfective. Present forms include гарантирую and гарантирует. It takes an accusative object; ничего is genitive under negation. The present with пока withholds a guarantee for now. The speaker agrees to discuss a discount without promising one. |
| ru-04-1325 | B2n | provide; Perfective infinitive after прошу. Perfective; imperfective предоставлять. Future forms include предоставлю and предоставит. It takes an accusative item and can name its recipient in the dative; the infinitive requests one completed provision. The guest asks the administrator for a specific remedy. |
| ru-04-1326 | B2n | will not manage in time; Negated first-person singular present. Imperfective; perfective успеть. Present forms include успеваю and успевает. | ru-04-1328 | B2n | only; Лишь limits the customer's acceptance to the delivery condition that follows. |
| ru-04-1329 | B2n | a different; Accusative singular masculine inanimate, agreeing with способ. Иной contrasts the proposed method with the one already under discussion. |
| ru-04-1330 | B2n | extra; Nominative singular feminine, agreeing with услуга. Лишняя identifies a service the customer considers wrongly included in the bill. |
| ru-04-1331 | B2n | an alternative; Accusative singular feminine, direct object of рассмотрим. The alternative is another option to consider before approving the plan. |

Взять на себя takes an accusative responsibility or cost; the future commits the speaker to accepting it. Взять стоимость доставки на себя means that the seller will pay the delivery cost. |
| ru-04-1333 | B2n | I cannot undertake to promise; Negated first-person singular present reflexive verb followed by an infinitive. Браться is imperfective; perfective взяться. Present берётся. It takes an infinitive naming the undertaking. Обещать is imperfective; perfective пообещать. Present обещает. It takes an accusative object; the present phrase withholds a promise for now. The speaker cautiously declines to promise a launch date before the resource decision. |
| ru-04-1334 | B2n | allow me to disagree; Разрешите is a plural imperative used for polite singular address; согласиться is a reflexive infinitive. Согласиться is perfective, with imperfective соглашаться; future соглашусь, согласится. Here the formula introduces an immediate objection. The formula politely signals disagreement with the addressee before giving a reason. |
| ru-04-1335 | B2n | or; Либо links the two remedies offered to the customer. |
| ru-04-1336 | B2n | with a reservation; С governs instrumental singular feminine оговоркой. The phrase qualifies acceptance of the plan; the following clause states the reservation. It does not mean a slip of the tongue or specified contract terms. |

The employee offers to take a new task on condition that the boss removes the previous one. |
| ru-04-1338 | B2n | valid; Short neuter singular adjective, agreeing with замечание. The speaker accepts the colleagues' criticism as justified. |

| ru-04-1340 | B2n | while retaining; Perfective adverbial participle; its understood actor is the group invited to shorten the text. Perfective; imperfective сохранять. This controlled written construction states what must be retained in the proposed cut. |

Оставаться открытым means that the question has not yet been settled. |

| ru-04-1345 | B2n | a definite; Accusative singular feminine, agreeing with дату. Конкретную дату asks for a definite date after repeated postponements. |
| ru-04-1346 | B2n | pilot; Genitive singular masculine, agreeing with проекта after с. Пилотный проект is a limited trial before a possible larger rollout. |
| ru-04-1347 | B2n | only until lunchtime; До governs genitive singular обеда. Только limits the employee's stated willingness to work on Saturday. |
| ru-04-1348 | B2n | at your discretion; На governs accusative singular neuter усмотрение; ваше agrees with it. The supervisor delegates the choice of meeting format to the colleague addressed. |
| ru-04-1349 | B2n | are obliged; Short plural adjective, agreeing with мы and followed by an infinitive. Вынуждены presents the refusal as compelled by the stated expired deadline. |
| ru-04-1350 | B2n | do not take this as a reproach; Negated second-person plural imperative, used for polite singular address; за governs accusative singular упрёк. Счесть is perfective; imperfective считать. Its future is irregular: сочту, сочтёт. Счесть что за что takes an accusative object and за with the accusative; the criticism is supplied by the following clause. The negated imperative mitigates its attribution as reproach. This formal preface softens the criticism that follows without withdrawing it. |
| ru-04-1351 | B2n | I request assistance; Прошу is first-person singular present; содействия is genitive singular neuter. Просить is imperfective; perfective попросить. It belongs to the second conjugation; compare просит. Просить содействия takes the genitive; the present performs the request. The speaker formally asks the administrator for help establishing the repair date after a leak. |
| ru-04-1352 | B2n | priority; Nominative singular masculine, subject of the clause. The contrast identifies fixing the error as the group's immediate priority. |
| ru-04-1353 | B2n | a deferral; Nominative singular feminine, subject of устроит. Отсрочка платежа means a delay in the payment deadline; the customer offers it as an alternative to a discount. |

| ru-04-1355 | B2n | reconsider; Perfective infinitive after прошу. Perfective; imperfective пересматривать. Future forms include пересмотрю and пересмотрит. | ru-04-1356 | B2n | final; Accusative singular neuter, agreeing with решение. Окончательное решение identifies the decision that settles the matter. |

| ru-04-1358 | B2n | objections to; Возражений is genitive plural after нет; против governs the genitive версии. The speaker accepts the trial version in principle while objecting to an immediate launch. |
| ru-04-1359 | B2n | does not match; Negated third-person singular present, agreeing with товар. Imperfective; no simple perfective partner in this sense. Present forms include соответствую and соответствует. Соответствовать takes the dative; the present describes the product's mismatch with its description. Не соответствует описанию gives the reason for requesting a refund. |
| ru-04-1360 | B2n | record; First-person plural perfective future used with давайте as a proposal. Perfective; imperfective фиксировать. Future forms include зафиксирую and зафиксируем. | ru-04-1361 | B2n | object; Perfective infinitive after вынуждена. Perfective; imperfective возражать. Future forms include возражу́ and возрази́т. Возразить can introduce the content of an objection; here the infinitive names an objection made now. Вынуждена возразить introduces a direct objection as necessary in the circumstances. |
| ru-04-1362 | B2n | worries; Third-person singular present; меня is the accusative person affected. Imperfective; perfective побеспокоить can describe causing concern on one occasion. Present forms include беспоко́ю and беспоко́ит. It takes an accusative person and here introduces a что-clause expressing a current concern. Меня беспокоит frames the objection as the employee's concern. |
| ru-04-1363 | B2n | reject; Imperfective infinitive after не спешите. Imperfective; perfective отвергнуть. Present forms include отверга́ю and отверга́ет. | ru-04-1364 | B2n | remedy; Perfective infinitive after предлагаем. Perfective; imperfective устранять. Future forms include устраню́ and устрани́т. | ru-04-1365 | B2n | will make a concession; First-person plural perfective future. Perfective; imperfective уступать. Future forms include уступлю́ and уступи́т. Уступить в цене means to make a price concession in the negotiation. |
| ru-04-1366 | B2n | deserves consideration; Third-person singular present; внимания is genitive singular. Imperfective; perfective заслужить. Present forms include заслу́живаю and заслу́живает. Заслуживать takes the genitive in this expression; the present assesses the idea now. Заслуживает внимания gives qualified encouragement without approving the idea. |

Принять меры takes accusative меры; the infinitive asks for action to be taken. Принять меры requests a remedy without specifying the exact steps. |
| ru-04-1368 | B2n | acceptable; Short plural adjective, agreeing with расходы. Допустимы asks which expenses fall within the team's acceptable limits. |
| ru-04-1369 | B2n | excuse; Perfective infinitive after прошу. Perfective; imperfective освобождать. Future forms include освобожу́ and освободи́т. Освободить кого от чего takes an accusative person and от with the genitive; the request concerns one duty. Освободить от дежурства asks the boss to excuse the employee from the duty. |
| ru-04-1370 | B2n | rests with you; Third-person singular present reflexive verb; за governs instrumental вами. Imperfective; perfective остаться. Present forms include остаю́сь and остаётся. Оставаться за кем takes за with the instrumental; the present assigns the choice to the client. Выбор остаётся за вами leaves the decision to the person addressed. |
| ru-04-1371 | B2n | would not; Negated feminine singular past with visible бы; masculine не стал is also accepted because the speaker's gender is not otherwise specified. Perfective стать; imperfective становиться is not a direct partner in this modal use. Future forms include ста́ну and ста́нет. Не стать with an infinitive and бы gives a tentative recommendation against an action. Не стала бы исключать suggests keeping the online option available. |
| ru-04-1372 | B2n | compensate; Infinitive after прошу. Компенсировать can be perfective or imperfective; here it is perfective. Finite forms include компенси́рую and компенси́рует. It takes an accusative object; the infinitive requests one payment as compensation. The customer requests reimbursement of the delivery cost because the order was late. |
